# Current bibliography and source reconciliation

The inventory accounts for **all 630 current bibliography keys**. Each has its original target fields, current recognized citation locations, identified manifestations or an explicit holdings gap, documented reading/review evidence, and a concrete remaining source obligation in [the companion JSON](<C:/Dev/the-methods-of-reason/spec/inventory/2026-09-06/bibliography-source-reconciliation.json>). There are no remaining key sets or targeted identity checks in this scoped inventory.

The live bibliography is unchanged: SHA-256 `d5bab1c74ab2b378eaacafe2ce1b313209e71e46a28dd2748ef7c539c5cb7094`. All 630 field dictionaries agree with the current key inventory. This report records source holdings and evidence; it does not measure manuscript writing or establish whole-source mathematical acceptance.

## Reconciled states

| State | Keys |
| --- | ---: |
| No local body or matching source record identified | 80 |
| Records or references; no target body identified | 456 |
| Partial source evidence | 14 |
| Selected body identified | 58 |
| Work held in a distinct version | 19 |
| Related source held; exact target body absent | 3 |

These states count bibliography targets with different sizes and purposes: an entire book, a journal article, an author draft, a chapter, and a maintained manual each count as one entry. They cannot be used as a writing percentage. A body can be held while most of its contents remain unread; a bounded primary-source reading can be documented even when no local PDF was preserved.

**Citation mechanisms:** 615 keys occur in parsed citation commands or recognized editorial/card mechanisms, and 15 do not. These are not counts of every human-readable source mention. All 840 active LaTeX files were searched separately for literal keys and normalized exact titles for the 15 zero-count entries. Fourteen had no match. For `BalbesDwinger1974`, “distributive lattices” occurs only as a generic topic phrase at [handbook/parts/p10/priestley-duality.tex:3](<C:/Dev/the-methods-of-reason/handbook/parts/p10/priestley-duality.tex:3>), lines 40 and 103 as well; none names that publication. Author-name allusions are not asserted exhaustively resolved. Bibliography retention is distinguished from source use in every zero-count row.

## Findings that change or refine the narrower PDF candidate inventory

- **Published Description Logics chapter identified:** [tmp/acquisition/src036/uniba-handbook-of-kr.pdf](<C:/Dev/the-methods-of-reason/tmp/acquisition/src036/uniba-handbook-of-kr.pdf>) contains Baader–Horrocks–Sattler 2008 at physical pp. 164–208, printed pp. 135–179. Physical p. 164 prints the exact DOI `10.1016/S1574-6526(07)03003-9`, title and authors; p. 208 closes its references. The title/copyright material identifies the book's first edition, 2008. The previously reviewed 47-page 2007 author draft remains separate and keeps its own theorem/page locators. No new full-chapter fidelity review is claimed.
- **Baader–Snyder has body copies beyond the contents excerpt:** the 88-page [tmp/acquisition/src037/snyder-unification-pdf-retry-02.body](<C:/Dev/the-methods-of-reason/tmp/acquisition/src037/snyder-unification-pdf-retry-02.body>) and the 89-page existing PostScript derivative identify *Unification Theory*, Baader and Snyder, with the 2001 handbook imprint. Their printed endpoints/contents differ from both one another and the cited pp. 445–532. They are identified author chapter states, not silently accepted as exact published pagination.
- **W3C is held as HTML:** the dated Profiles, Structural Specification and Direct Semantics Recommendations are complete identified saved HTML pages, with their hashes matching the source note. The errata page is a separate saved snapshot. Profiles is recorded as read in full; only the specified companion sections were consulted. These files are not PDFs or snapshots of every linked document.
- **Other bodies missed by title matching:** Kozen 1983, the 1959 Büchi report, Kamp's 1968 thesis, Pnueli's printed 1977 body in later archival packaging, the two Russian Trakhtenbrot publications, Esakia 1974, Shepherdson–Sturgis 1963, Elgot's 1962 errata, and McMillan's 1992 thesis are individually identified. Büchi 1962 is complete on physical pp. 12–22 within a partial proceedings preview, in the 1977 Stanford printing. The enclosing preview is not the whole proceedings volume.
- **PostScript has source standing:** Safra's complete 20-page 1995 expansion is held in its actual PostScript format and is separately keyed. It does not supply the nine-page 1988 FOCS publication. Pnueli, Gelfond–Lifschitz, Pitts and Barendregt also have identified PostScript manifestations or existing derivatives. A PostScript DSC page count alone was not used as a body-fidelity certificate.
- **Saved web text is retained at its actual extent:** Rocq 9.1.1 and Agda 2.8.0 index extracts, the opening Agda manual excerpt through source physical p. 12, Lean latest-version contents/extract presentations, Isabelle2025-2 catalogue data, and HoTT arXiv front matter/contents evidence are recorded. They do not constitute the complete selected manuals/books. The Lean captures mention 4.34.0-rc1/4.33.0-rc1, while the bibliography selects 4.32.1.
- **Recorded remote reading remains visible:** Gallier's 2015 second edition and Silver's 1975 ICM pages were expressly read in memory without a local source download. Walukiewicz, Janin–Walukiewicz, Arnold, Shelah and Ciesielski have bounded primary-source reading records with explicit locators. Those records support their stated reading scope and do not create local PDF holdings.
- **False positives stay excluded:** `boolos-gl.pdf` is R. J. Buehler's 2014 notes. Burris's held text is the 2012 update. The Thomas “1997” filename holds the 1996 report. CVs, publication lists, bibliographic exports, other works' references, and generated manuscript PDFs do not supply target bodies. All 28 Word files are brief citation exports. The nine embedded archive PDFs duplicate already inventoried physical identities.

## Scope and evidence custody

The reconciliation reuses [all 106 candidate-set decisions](<C:/Dev/the-methods-of-reason/spec/inventory/2026-09-06/bibliography-pdf-identity-resolutions.json>), [all 24 image/source identities](<C:/Dev/the-methods-of-reason/spec/inventory/2026-09-06/image-only-source-identities.json>), and [the 16 selected source packets](<C:/Dev/the-methods-of-reason/spec/inventory/2026-09-06/source-holdings.json>). Existing source notes and reviews retain their historical/scoped standing. The 24-row packet includes the completed DAIMI PB-429 and BRICS RS-94-12 endpoint checks, with no claim of interior body fidelity from endpoint matching.

- **current_bibliography:** All 630 exact target field dictionaries, literal normalized entry hashes/lines, and current recognized citation locations. Live bibliography hash rechecked.
- **all_pdf_identities:** All 474 physical PDFs /406 byte identities from all 64947-file header scan; existing first-four-page text/metadata considered. Textless identities resolved in separate 24-row packet. Low-threshold supplementary title/DOI search produced 246 additional reference/discovery matches for 45 keys; never score-based acceptance.
- **prior_candidate_review:** All 106 prior candidate sets /197 candidate associations /125 byte identities and 6 supplemental holdings, preserving published/author/reprint/other-version/preview/false-positive decisions.
- **image_identity_review:** All 24 no-opening-text byte identities (33 physical copies):18 full source manifestations,3 partial excerpts,3 non-source conversion artifacts. DAIMI and BRICS endpoint checks complete; no interior body fidelity inferred.
- **note_index:** 339 source/research/provenance note metadata objects. Exact key/DOI occurrences for 460 keys; supplemental normalized-title search identified 134 further key-to-note sets (at most 2 representative notes retained perkey). Notes <=10 MB searched as full text; larger notes limited to first 128 KiB; giant coverage baseline metadata-only. Per-note parsed-prefix SHA 256 and matched lines retained. Lexical matches are not semantic/full-body acceptance.
- **non_pdf_formats:** 42 PostScript files;12 gzip streams (11 PS/1 DVI);28 Word files with 103–166 character citation exports;30 archives;9 embeddedPDFmembers all matching existing physical PDFs. Existing saved derivatives/title context used; no conversion/rendering or acquisition.
- **bounded_text_web:** 4452 local text/HTML/XML/body/response files,80 bytes–5 MB, at most 32768 bytes each; excluded 7715 diagnostic/page-derivative paths and 96 document containers.281 keys/2117 match occurrences; top 2 representative candidates retained perkey with prefix hash and semantic disposition. Larger/legacy catalogs were not fully scanned. Directly identified HTML/manual excerpts received full file hashes and exact extent descriptions.
- **zero_citation_mentions:** All 840 active LaTeX files searched for literal keys and exact normalized titles for 15 keys with zero recognized citations. No source-specific key/title use found; BalbesDwinger 1974 had only three generic topic-phrase matches.

The JSON records 162 fresh full-file hashes for used source representatives, notes and input artifacts; all compared source hashes matched. Other evidence uses the exact hash and parsed-prefix scope from its existing structured inventory. The two very large legacy coverage catalogs were not scanned. No new source download, source conversion, software test, manuscript build, or render was performed. Only the two assigned reconciliation outputs were written.

## Reading and remaining obligations

81 keys have an explicitly attached scoped reading/review record, including records of a source boundary or unsuccessful body acquisition. This count is not a count of complete works read or accepted. Every JSON row separately identifies its holdings state, exact-version gap, recorded reading scope and remaining book-source obligation.

For a selected full body, the remaining work is to preserve the identified version and keep each manuscript use within the recorded passage/review scope. For a distinct version, the edition/reprint/author-state distinction must remain explicit. For partial material, missing definitions, rules, examples and proof passages cannot be inferred from contents or adjacent works. For a holdings gap, an exact retained manifestation or reproducible primary-source access and passage-level evidence remains to be established where the book uses that source. Absence here is not a claim that the publication does not exist or cannot be downloaded.

A displayed representative prefers an exact selected manifestation; where none is identified, it prefers a complete identified work version over an excerpt. Other manifestations and their qualifications remain in the JSON.
